OpenWhispr/openwhispr ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.12.21.12.2 Stable2026-11-25T14:07:24Z

OpenWhispr 1.12.2

Windows hotfix: OpenWhispr now starts on PCs with Smart App Control or an App Control for Business policy. Before, Windows blocked one of the app's files and it stopped at launch with "An Application Control policy has blocked this file". If that happened to you, download the installer, since the app can't update itself while it can't start.

This release also brings subfolders, a mic mute for note recordings and snippet date variables, and fixes a Linux file handle leak.

🌟 Highlights

  • 📁 Subfolders — Folders can hold one level of subfolders, nested in the notes sidebar and synced across devices.
  • 🔇 Mute your mic during a note recording — Keep a side conversation out of the notes while system audio keeps transcribing.
  • 📅 Date and time in snippets — {{date}}, {{time}}, {{datetime}} and {{locale}} fill in each time a snippet is inserted.
  • 🏢 Outside guest controls — Organizations can block transcription of meetings that include outside guests.

🛠 Fixes

  • Linux: dictation no longer leaks file handles that made other apps crash or stall.
  • The voice assistant works again with Gemini Live transcription.
  • ElevenLabs Note Recording keeps every word and stops in under half a second.
  • Anthropic: Disable thinking is honored, and notes retry when a response runs out of tokens.
  • The note editor keeps Tab in the note and shows the toolbar only over selected text.
  • A .txt export of a note with only an AI summary is no longer empty, and notes are saved as files after a tray launch.
  • Custom cleanup endpoints whose API isn't under /v1 work.

Full details in the changelog.

🙏 Thanks

Thanks to @boseq, @pawisoon, @chirag127, @Chadpiha, @greatcoat, @stantheman0128, @orshih6, @widechaos and @dawarravi. 🎉

earendil-works/pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.6.1 Stable2026-11-25T13:41:35Z

New Features

  • Decision models in codemode: GitHub Copilot's gpt-6-luna-decisions and Radius decision models can be called from codemode models.classify(), and results include the provider's responseId. See Classify.
  • One setting per transcript notice: cache misses, cache warming, compaction costs, and reasoning drops each have their own setting under Notices in /settings. See Model and thinking.
  • Dialog status kind for extensions: ctx.ui.select(), ctx.ui.confirm(), and ctx.ui.input() accept blockedKind, so terminals with program status can show a dialog as a permission request, question, or login. See Program Status Kind.

Breaking Changes

  • Replaced the showCacheMissNotices setting with one setting per transcript notice: notices.cacheMisses, notices.cacheWarming, notices.compactionCosts, and notices.reasoningDrops, configured under Notices in /settings. An existing showCacheMissNotices value is migrated to all four. SettingsManager.getShowCacheMissNotices() and setShowCacheMissNotices() are removed; use getNotices() and setNotice() instead

Added

  • Added compat.supportsStrictMode for google-generative-ai and google-vertex models in models.json, so Gemini 3+ models served under IDs without the version can use strict tools (#12321)
  • Added a blockedKind option to ctx.ui.select(), ctx.ui.confirm() and ctx.ui.input() that sets the kind of blocked program status (OSC 7501) the dialog reports, for example "permission" for a select that approves an action. See Dialogs
  • Added full_output_error to the bash tool's structured output and fullOutputError to its details, BashResult and BashExecutionMessage, giving the reason when truncated output could not be saved to a temp file. User bash output that was truncated without a saved file now says so in the model context and the TUI (#10585)
  • Added the gpt-6-luna-decisions decision model for GitHub Copilot, callable from codemode models.classify() with text and images (#12395)
  • Added decision models on the Radius provider, callable from codemode models.classify()
  • Added responseId to codemode models.classify() results. OpenRouter's System One fills it with its generation ID, and its billed cost replaces the catalog estimate. See codemode

Changed

  • Changed the default Mistral model from devstral-medium-latest to mistral-medium-latest, because Mistral retired Devstral and the model catalog no longer lists it
  • Changed after_provider_response to also fire for HTTP error responses (4xx/5xx) and for each retried attempt on OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google models, as it already did for OpenAI Codex, Mistral, Radius, and Amazon Bedrock, so extensions can read the status and retry-after of rate-limited requests (#12376)
  • Changed first-time setup to show the colored pi logo and only ask about analytics; the theme defaults to the system theme, which follows the terminal's colors

Fixed

  • Fixed selecting the xhigh thinking level for Gemini 4 Hydrogen and Neon on the google and google-vertex providers failing with Unsupported Google thinking level mapping ... xhigh -> xhigh (#12184)
  • Fixed sessions with very many entries after the last compaction (about 120k or more) failing to load with RangeError: Maximum call stack size exceeded when building the context (#12154)
  • Fixed ctx.ui.pasteToEditor() and ctx.ui.setEditorText() not repainting the editor when called outside terminal input handling, such as after an await in a command handler (#12295)
  • Fixed the bash tool returning (no output) when the output ended with a line over 50KB followed by an empty line; it now shows the end of that line (#12350)
  • Fixed /tree navigation adding the branch's prompts to Up-arrow history again on every navigation; prompts already in history now move to the most recent position instead of repeating (#12279)
  • Fixed closing the console window or terminal tab on Windows skipping session_shutdown and leaving commands started by the bash tool running, for example sleep 300 from bash -c "sleep 300". pi now handles SIGHUP on Windows like on POSIX and waits for taskkill to finish before it exits. Quitting with Ctrl+D or /quit while a ! command runs, or ending RPC mode while a bash command runs, no longer leaves that command running on Windows either
  • Fixed the AuthStorage coalesced-reload test failing intermittently on systems with coarse file timestamps (#12190)
  • Fixed the thinkingLevelMap reference in the models.json schema and docs suggesting pi does not support the xhigh and max thinking levels; it now says they are opt-in and only offered when mapped, and the model guide explains how to map thinking levels (#12281)
  • Fixed Mermaid diagrams wider than the terminal falling back to their source without explanation; a note now says how many columns the diagram needs (#12324)
  • Fixed copies on Wayland silently failing when wl-copy guessed a non-text MIME type from the copied text, for example application/x-ruby for text starting with def, which clipboard managers such as Klipper discard; pi now copies as text/plain (#12446 by @ttempaa)
  • Fixed pi crashing when the bash tool or a ! command could not write its full-output temp file, for example when /tmp was full, over a per-user quota or not writable; both now keep the truncated output and say why the full output could not be saved. Opening the external editor and /share now report such failures instead of crashing (#10585)
  • Fixed /login showing a provider as configured when its stored auth.json key is a !command that fails, while requests failed with No API key found. /login now shows stored key did not resolve, getProviderAuthStatus() returns { configured: false, source: "stored" }, and entering the same !command again in /login runs it again instead of reusing the cached failure (#12164)
  • Fixed pi update run from WSL through the Windows installation of pi rebuilding that installation with Linux npm; it now stops and says that running the Windows installation from WSL is unsupported (#12172)
  • Fixed right-click paste inserting the clipboard twice in Tabby on Windows, which pastes on right-click itself (#12406)
  • Fixed dialogs shown before interactive mode starts, such as the project trust prompt, the missing session folder prompt and first-time setup, reporting no blocked program status (OSC 7501). Blocked messages of multi-line dialog titles are now the first line only, for example Trust project folder? instead of the folder path and explanation. The project trust prompt and first-time setup report kind=permission (#12439)
  • Fixed GitHub Copilot listing decision models the account cannot use, such as microsoft-decision-1 on accounts whose plan does not include it, where every call failed with HTTP 400. After /login or the next token refresh, only the decision models in the account's Copilot model catalog are listed (#12395)
  • Fixed the bash tool on Windows not finding Git for Windows installed outside Program Files and running commands through the WSL launcher System32\bash.exe instead. pi now asks the git.exe on PATH for its installation (git var GIT_SHELL_PATH, Git 2.47+) and uses its Git Bash before other bash.exe on PATH (#12408)
  • Fixed RPC mode, session loading, and llama.cpp model events rescanning an incomplete line on every chunk, which made large lines, such as messages with images, take time quadratic in their size. llama.cpp model events also accept lone CR line endings and CRLF split across chunks (#12224)
  • Fixed ! command output coming back collapsed after compaction, /reload, /resume or /tree while ctrl+o was set to expanded (#12426, #12427 by @acmerfight)
  • Fixed more temporary provider failures not being retried automatically: Cloudflare HTTP 525, gateway "provider is temporarily unavailable" and partial-response errors, Anthropic api_error events during streaming, OpenRouter empty responses and stops without output, streams ending with finish_reason: "error", and "request rate increased too quickly" throttling (#12206, #12227, #12240, #12407)
  • Fixed unrelated request errors whose code contains request_too_large inside a longer identifier, such as plugin_request_too_large, triggering compact-and-retry as if the context overflowed (#12343, #12438 by @muyiyr)
  • Fixed HTTP 400 with thinking off on Claude Sonnet 5.5 through GitHub Copilot, and on Claude Opus 5.5 and Sonnet 5.5 through Cloudflare AI Gateway and Vercel AI Gateway; selecting off on those models now uses the lowest supported level (#12429)
  • Fixed Gemini 3+ models whose IDs carry a prefix, such as vendor-gemini-4-argon, being treated as older Gemini models: they now get strict tools, tool-call IDs, and images inside function responses (#12321)
  • Fixed Google prompt blocks being reported and retried as incomplete streams; the error now includes the block reason and safety ratings (#12330)
  • Fixed openai-codex starting GPT-6 requests at medium regardless of the selected thinking level (#12361)
  • Fixed GPT-6.2 dropping earlier reasoning as prefix_mismatch after the OpenAI Codex WebSocket connection expired (#12367)
  • Fixed Amazon Bedrock requests hanging behind HTTPS_PROXY with AWS SSO profiles (#12353)
  • Fixed HTTP 400 after tool calls without text on Cloudflare Workers AI's GPT-OSS 120B and 20B, Granite 4.0 H Micro, and Llama 3.3 70B Instruct FP8 Fast (#12194)
  • Fixed HTTP 400 for non-strict Anthropic tools with Draft 7 tuples (#12212)
  • Fixed OpenAI Decisions usage dropping cache read and write tokens (#12264)
  • Fixed extension providers with fetchModels making model lookups quadratic in catalog size, which blocked pi for about a second with ~10k models (#12311)
  • Fixed the model list still offering models their providers shut down: gpt-5-chat-latest on openai and azure, Groq's llama-3.1-8b-instant, llama-3.3-70b-versatile and qwen/qwen3.6-27b, and the undocumented mistral-medium-3.5 (#12202)
  • Fixed Markdown after an unclosed $ before a number, such as +$50, rendering as raw text for the rest of the paragraph (#12389 by @wuhaoyujerry)
  • Fixed rendering a bare URL that runs into a multi-megabyte line exhausting the heap in terminals with OSC 8 hyperlinks (#12416)
  • Fixed MCP requests to servers that echo request IDs as strings timing out, and large MCP Streamable HTTP events blocking pi for seconds (#12205, #12224)

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v4.0.42CC Switch v4.0.42 Stable2026-11-25T13:27:43Z

CC Switch v4.0.42

本版本让 GitHub Copilot 供应商支持 Copilot 的 Auto 自动选模型：在 Claude、Codex 或 Claude Desktop 的模型列表里选「GitHub Copilot 自动选择」，每次对话由 Copilot 决定用哪个模型，Copilot Free 账号也能用。

本版本还修复了 4.0.41 引入的问题：所有应用的供应商卡片都显示「agy 在用」，副标题也被换成 Antigravity 账号邮箱。另外，Antigravity 官方卡换号时，系统钥匙串第一次读回失败不会再把已经换好的账号绑定撤销。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

GitHub Copilot Auto 自动选模型

（#9564，见 #8197）

Copilot 上游调整了 Auto 的接入方式，之前省略模型名的做法已经不能用。现在 CC Switch 会先向 Copilot 的 /auto 询问这次该用哪个模型，再带着 Copilot 给的会话令牌向那个模型发请求。

  • 怎么用：在 Copilot 供应商的表单里点「获取模型列表」，账号支持 Auto 时列表里会多一项「GitHub Copilot 自动选择」（模型 ID copilot-fusion-auto），选它即可。Claude Code、Codex 和 Claude Desktop 都可以选，Claude Desktop 可以只给某一档填它、其他档照常填具体模型。
  • Copilot Free 也能用：Free 账号的模型目录里没有标明 Auto 能力，CC Switch 会替这个账号试问一次 /auto（只让它选模型，不发对话），能选出来就显示 Auto 选项。
  • 同一段对话尽量用同一个模型：Copilot 选出的模型在授权有效期内会接着用。闲置超过 30 分钟、授权到期、账号换了，或者上下文压缩成功之后，会重新问一次，这时可能换成别的模型。
  • 选不出来时：Copilot 拒绝或选出的模型不能用当前协议时，请求会报错，开了故障转移的会切到下一家。选固定模型的请求和以前一样。

需要开启本地路由。详见用户手册 GitHub Copilot Auto。

修复

  • 所有供应商卡片都显示「agy 在用」（#9975，关闭 #9981）：4.0.41 里，打开过 Antigravity 页或授权中心之后，其他应用的卡片、Antigravity 的中转卡片也会显示「agy 在用」的绿色标签，副标题被换成 Antigravity 账号邮箱。现在只有 Antigravity 官方卡才显示账号。
  • Antigravity 官方卡换号后绑定被撤销（#9979）：系统钥匙串有时写入了新登录，第一次读回却报错，CC Switch 会把卡片的账号绑定撤销，实际上 agy 已经换到了新账号。现在会再读一次；还是读不到时，提示登录已经写入，请先到授权中心看当前登录的是哪个账号再重试。

致谢

感谢 @Railgun-wiki（Copilot Auto）、@allenxu09（Copilot Auto、Antigravity 卡片修复）的贡献。

感谢 @wan-zhao 报告问题。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

v4.0.41CC Switch v4.0.41 Stable2026-11-25T09:59:03Z

CC Switch v4.0.41

本版本新增 CodeBuddy 支持（腾讯的 CLI 和桌面版共用一套配置）：可以管理自定义模型、MCP、Skills、提示词、会话和用量，在授权中心收集多个官方账号，查看套餐、积分和到期时间并随时切换。Copilot CLI 的供应商可以一键同步到 VS Code 的 Copilot 插件；Grok 官方卡显示订阅等级。

本版本还让 Claude 原生转发复用上游连接，主窗口和托盘面板启动时加载的代码更少，后台请求和供应商连通性检查失败时会说明原因，并加固了数据库文件权限、备份文件名、CONNECT 代理和深链接的安全检查。另外修复了 Codex 路由到 Opus / Sonnet 5.5 时工具调用后报「requires adaptive thinking」；Gemini Native 工具调用历史较长时报 400；旧请求的故障转移结果覆盖手动选的路由；Antigravity 卡片绑定的账号和授权中心不同步；Codex 分叉会话的用量一直不入账；Pi 插件被当成 pnpm 处理；Windows 上 Hermes 显示两处安装；macOS 更新后 Dock 栏多出一个图标等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

CodeBuddy

新增 CodeBuddy（#8758）。CodeBuddy 的桌面版和 CLI 读同一套配置文件，所以界面上统一叫 CodeBuddy。它和 Kimi Code、WorkBuddy 一样是累加式，多个模型可以同时添加。

  • 自定义模型：每张卡是 ~/.codebuddy/models.json 里的一个模型，卡片 ID 就是发给上游的模型名，保存后不能改，也不提供「复制」。可以从地址获取模型列表，选中模型后自动填好 token 上限和能力。文件里其他模型和你自己加的字段都保留；带注释或尾逗号的文件不会被改写；settings.json 里设为默认的模型不能移除。新增 DeepSeek、小米 MiMo、LongCat 预设。
  • MCP、Skills、提示词：MCP 写进 CodeBuddy 实际读取的那一个用户级文件，保留你自己的服务器；在 CC Switch 里启用的服务器会从 CodeBuddy 的停用列表里去掉。Skills 同步到 ~/.codebuddy/skills，不会删掉不是 CC Switch 装的技能。提示词写进 CODEBUDDY.md。
  • 会话和用量：会话列表里能看 CodeBuddy 的会话并在终端里恢复（不能在 CC Switch 里删除），用量统计会导入主会话和子 agent 的用量，子 agent 的用量算在所属会话上。
  • 官方账号：授权中心可以收进当前登录的 CodeBuddy 账号，或在终端里登录一个新账号；CodeBuddy 已登录时，会先确认，再让它显示登录界面，取消登录会还原原来的号。新增「CodeBuddy 官方登录」卡，绑定一个账号，点「添加」即切换到这个号，授权中心账号的「…」菜单也能切换。账号行和卡片显示套餐、本期积分和到期时间。账号副本只存在本机，不参与同步。
  • 「应用」页：可以安装、更新和卸载 CodeBuddy CLI。

Copilot CLI 同步到 VS Code Copilot 插件

在「设置 → 应用配置 → Copilot CLI」打开「应用到 VS Code Copilot 插件」（#9435，关闭 #9433），Copilot CLI 里添加的供应商和模型会写进 VS Code Copilot 插件的模型配置，两边各自在模型列表里选用。

  • 自动找到 VS Code 正式版和 Insiders 的默认及命名 Profile，全部同步；之后在 CC Switch 里添加、编辑、移除 Copilot CLI 供应商时自动更新。
  • 只改 CC Switch 写的那一组，其他配置和注释都保留；第一次修改前会在旁边留一份备份。关闭开关会删掉这一组。
  • 同步失败时，Copilot CLI 那边的保存照常生效，提示里写明原因并提供「重试」。
  • WebSocket、带 apiVersion 的 Azure 部署等少数配置暂不支持，遇到时会提示原因。

Grok 官方卡显示订阅等级

Grok Build 的官方卡在账号后面显示订阅等级，例如 SuperGrok Heavy（#9923）。绑定了 xAI 账号的卡也可以「配置用量查询」，设置自动刷新间隔；关掉用量查询时订阅等级一起隐藏。

变更

  • Claude 原生转发复用上游连接（#9914，关闭 #9913）：本地路由原样转发 Claude 请求时，不再每次都重新建立到上游的连接，直连和经代理的连接都会复用。复用的旧连接失败时自动换新连接重发；经代理时只在还没收到任何数据的情况下换新连接。
  • 启动更轻（#9918、#9920）：界面语言包改为只加载当前用的语言；托盘面板的「用量」页签切过去才加载，面板收起时停止后台刷新，托盘面板启动时加载的代码从约 3.5 MB 减到约 0.5 MB。后端计算用量中位数时少分配内存，用不到的网络客户端不再提前创建。
  • 网络失败时说明原因（见 #9911）：启动时的后台请求失败会提示一次是哪个请求：检查更新会写明访问的是 dl.ccswitch.io / github.com，models.dev 同步会说明是为了获取价格计算用量费用，提示里可以选「不再提示」或直接关掉自动同步。检查供应商连通性失败时会区分被防火墙拦截、端口没有服务、超时、DNS 和 TLS 错误，给出对应的建议。
  • 侧栏收起后更窄（#9941）：侧栏收起后的宽度和选中项的高亮变窄，「舒适」密度比「紧凑」略宽，图标大小不变。

安全

  • 数据库、备份、代理和深链接（#9835）：在 macOS / Linux 上，导出的 SQL 文件和 cc-switch.db 只有当前用户能读写。恢复、重命名、删除备份时，只接受单个 .db 文件名。经 HTTP 代理（CONNECT）连接上游时，代理地址里转义过的用户名密码会先还原再发送（修复带特殊字符时 407），并限制代理返回的响应头大小。深链接导入的风险检查会标出更多可被利用的环境变量，以及 node -e、python -c 这类直接执行代码的命令。

修复

  • Codex 路由到 Opus / Sonnet 5.5 时工具调用后报 400（见 #9943）：Codex 经本地路由用 Anthropic 接口的 Opus 5.5 或 Sonnet 5.5 时，工具调用之后的每一轮都报「requires adaptive thinking」。这两个模型不允许关闭思考，现在这种情况下改发自适应思考；Codex 设为 none 的思考强度按 low 发。其他模型的请求不变。
  • Gemini Native 工具调用历史较长时报 400（#9009）：对话超过 64 轮后，较早工具调用的签名会丢失，可能错配到别的调用，Gemini 拒收。现在签名单独缓存更久，并按调用 ID 精确对应；实在找不回签名时，只对 Gemini 3 及以上用 Google 文档里的兼容标记。
  • 旧请求的故障转移结果覆盖手动选的路由（#9946，关闭 #9944）：手动切换路由或关掉自动故障转移后，还在进行中的旧请求不会再把路由切回备用供应商，也不会显示成当前使用的供应商。
  • Antigravity 卡片绑定的账号和授权中心不同步（#9945）：在 Antigravity 官方卡上绑定或更换账号并保存时，会经授权中心切换 agy 的登录，切换失败则不保存；卡片、账号选择框和授权中心显示同一个当前账号。复制卡片不会切换账号。
  • Codex 分叉会话的用量一直不入账（#9638，关闭 #9637）：从闲置的会话分叉出来的旧版 Codex 会话，核对过复制来的历史属于父会话后即导入用量；判断不了的仍然暂缓。
  • 全局代理设置只生效一半（#9930）：连续保存两次全局代理时，部分请求可能走的是另一次的代理设置。现在代理地址和网络客户端一次整体替换。
  • Pi 插件被当成 pnpm 处理（#9964，见 #9960）：系统里找到的 pi 实际是 pnpm 时，插件页会显示 0 个插件，在「发现」里点安装还会往 ~/.pi/agent 里装 npm 包。现在认不出 Pi 的输出时直接报错。同时修正了工具路径识别：经 pnpm、mise、volta 这类快捷方式安装的工具不会被认错，终端启动脚本打印的路径不会被当成工具位置，macOS / Linux 上也会查找 pnpm 的全局安装目录。
  • Windows 上 Hermes 显示两处安装（见 #9931）：同一个目录里的 hermes.cmd 和 hermes.exe 现在只显示一处，按 Windows 实际会运行的那个算。
  • macOS 更新后 Dock 栏多出一个图标（#9910，关闭 #8329）：应用内更新或改配置目录后重启时，会等旧进程完全退出再启动新的，复用固定在 Dock 里的图标。
  • SQL 导入后同步失败也提示成功（#9541，关闭 #9536）：导入后同步提示词、价格和设置失败时，会显示对应的警告。
  • 云同步后 Skills 里的脚本不能执行（#9539，关闭 #9534）：经 WebDAV / S3 同步的 Skills 会保留脚本的可执行权限。
  • 再次打开设置时闪一下加载图标（#9908，关闭 #9907）：已有缓存时直接显示设置内容。
  • 用量热力图开头的月份重叠（见 #9917）：开头只剩一两列的月份不再标月份，不会和下个月挤在一起。
  • 授权中心刷新额度时进度条左右晃动：刷新时只转图标，不再换文字，那一列宽度不变。
  • WorkBuddy 手动刷新额度没反应：WorkBuddy 没运行，或登录的不是这个账号时，只能拿到上次的结果，现在会提示原因。

升级说明

  • 数据库版本从 27 升到 28，为 MCP 和 Skills 增加 CodeBuddy 的开关。升级前会自动备份数据库；升级后再退回旧版本，旧版本会提示数据库版本过新，打不开。
  • macOS 上从旧版本更新到 4.0.41 的这一次重启仍由旧版本执行，可能还会多出一个 Dock 图标；之后的更新和重启不会再出现。

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.9.120 Stable2026-11-25T13:21:18Z

A batch of resolution, serve, export, clustering and performance fixes across nine community pull requests. The Python function-local-import change alters node/edge output, so the AST cache schema bumps (55 to 56) and Python re-extracts on the first build after upgrading; the other fixes need no rebuild.

  • Feat: serve a new affected MCP tool reports the reverse-dependency blast radius of a symbol (what breaks if you change it), and query_graph gains an optional seeds argument to start a traversal from known nodes; both resolve a node by name, file path, or id the same way the CLI does (#5254, thanks @ArnabOPP; proposed by @ramarro123).
  • Perf: query per-node search fields, degrees and the hub threshold, an integer-indexed adjacency for BFS/DFS, and rendered NODE lines are each computed once per loaded graph and reused by every later query, and document frequency is taken through the trigram index; query output is byte-identical (#5476, thanks @nothariharan).
  • Fix: python a bare call inside a function whose name is bound by a function-local import (from m import x, import x) no longer binds to an unrelated same-named module-level definition in the same file; the import shadows the twin within that function only (#5427, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Fix: python a function-local import no longer leaks its binding into a sibling function at the same scope, so a same-named call elsewhere in the file resolves to its own target rather than the imported one (#5436, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Fix: watch the watcher test helper replaces the clock in watch.py only, instead of patching the process-wide time.sleep, which had broken the subprocess waits a rebuild performs and made the watch tests flaky under the parallel suite (#5444, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: serve an IPv6 bind host is bracketed in the printed streamable-HTTP URL (http://[::1]:8080/mcp), so the connection URL is valid (#5470, thanks @leninworld).
  • Fix: cluster cohesion_score is bounded to [0.0, 1.0] on a MultiGraph or DiGraph by counting unique connected node pairs rather than parallel or reciprocal edges, so the density invariant holds (#5459, thanks @Gaurav-meena95).
  • Fix: export the Cypher, GraphML and SVG exporters create missing destination parent directories (matching the other exporters), and the Cypher writer is atomic so an interrupted write cannot leave a truncated file (#5450, thanks @Gaurav-meena95).
  • Fix: export the Obsidian, canvas, Cypher and SVG exporters tolerate a node whose label is null or non-string instead of crashing (#5457, thanks @Gaurav-meena95).

v0.9.119 Stable2026-11-24T15:23:07Z

A fix for a core-install regression in 0.9.118, plus a batch of resolution, extractor, export and performance fixes across ten community pull requests. The Python, Go and COBOL changes alter node/edge output, so the AST cache schema bumps (53 to 55) and those languages re-extract on the first build after upgrading; the other fixes need no rebuild.

  • Fix: serve graph commands (query, path, explain, affected, benchmark) run again on a core install without the [mcp] extra. 0.9.118 imported anyio (an mcp-extra-only dependency) at the top of graphify/serve.py, so loading it raised ModuleNotFoundError for those commands; the import is moved back inside the MCP server path where anyio is always present (#5320, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: python a call to a name that a lambda parameter shadows no longer binds to a module-level definition inside the lambda body; the shadow is scoped to the lambda body only, so a genuine outer call elsewhere still resolves (#5417, thanks @melhusl-hs).
  • Fix: python a call made through a function parameter (the enclosing function's own parameter, or one captured from an outer function) is likewise no longer bound to a module-level definition of that name; the parameter is a runtime callback whose target is unknown (#5418, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: go a local variable that shadows an imported package name resolves as a local receiver, not the package, so its member calls bind to the local's type; the shadow is lexically scoped and an unknown local fails closed rather than falling back to a package call (#5409, thanks @sdivyanshu90).
  • Fix: go a type declared across sibling files in a package folds to one node per directory even when a same-named package exists elsewhere, so its member calls resolve instead of being dropped as ambiguous; the two same-named packages stay distinct (#5304 follow-up, thanks @PravalVerma).
  • Fix: cobol a GO TO now emits a calls edge to the paragraph it jumps to (including each target of GO TO ... DEPENDING ON), so a paragraph reached only by a jump is no longer orphaned; an unknown target fails closed (#5423, thanks @pavansystems).
  • Fix: wiki article bodies link cross-references by node ID rather than label, so two nodes sharing a label link to their own articles instead of collapsing onto the first-registered one; the link text stays the human-readable label (thanks @azizur100389).
  • Fix: affected the reverse traversal on an undirected graph walks incident edges instead of scanning the whole edge list, fixing a dropped-orientation bug (it returned nothing on an annotated undirected graph) and turning an O(edges) step into O(degree); the directed path that the CLI always uses is unchanged (#5437, thanks @Gaurav-meena95).
  • Perf: query a CLI full-graph scan skips query terms that are provably absent from the corpus (no trigram present), so a long question no longer wastes time scoring terms that can match nothing; query results are byte-identical (#5429, thanks @Max17190).
  • Perf: build file-label disambiguation for shared basenames is computed with a suffix counter instead of per-file all-pairs comparison, turning an O(files²) step into a near-linear one (~260x on a 2,000-file collision) with byte-identical labels (thanks @Gaurav-meena95).

  • ☁️ 原生云同步 (Native cloud sync): Google Drive 与 iCloud 同步均已接入 Safari 原生层。 Google Drive and iCloud sync are integrated through Safari's native layer.
  • 🔔 系统通知 (Notifications): Gemini 回复完成后可发送 macOS 通知，并直达对应对话。 macOS can notify you when a Gemini response finishes and open the matching conversation.
  • 🔄 自动更新 (Automatic updates): 完成这次迁移后，后续版本可通过 Sparkle 自动更新。 After this one-time migration, future versions can update automatically through Sparkle.

v16.1.3 Stable2026-11-24T18:32:35Z

Critical fix

  • OpenRouter errors sent with HTTP 200 no longer discard queued observations (#6448, thanks @ryrenz, closes #6447 reported by @claudemeister). OpenRouter can accept a request and then report a temporary failure ("high demand", "temporarily rate-limited upstream") in the body of a 200 response, as {"error":{"code":<number>,...}}. The observer classified these as unrecoverable, so it did not retry, pause or fall back: it finalized the session and discarded its queued observations. The reporter lost 8.5% to 16% of events in one day. The classifier now uses the numeric error.code when the HTTP status is 2xx, so code 429 is a rate limit and 5xx is transient. A litellm output failure ("Unable to get json") stays an output failure and is never resent, because the model already ran and was billed.

Fixes

  • claude-mem server jobs retry no longer reports requeued for a job it did not publish (#6439, thanks @HEOJUNFO). If Redis was unreachable, or the shell had no CLAUDE_MEM_QUEUE_ENGINE=bullmq, the command printed "outcome": "requeued" and exited 0, and the job never ran. It now moves the row back to failed with retry_republish_failed, prints the reason and exits 1, so the same command works once the queue is reachable.
  • Server generation jobs wait out a provider's Retry-After (#6440, thanks @HEOJUNFO). A rate-limited job was redelivered after 5s and 10s whatever wait the provider asked for, so all three attempts could land inside the window and the job failed with no observation. When the provider sends a wait, the job is now delayed for it (capped at 60s, the same cap the worker uses). Rate limits without a wait, timeouts and 5xx keep the 5s/10s backoff.
  • POST /v1/events answers 400 instead of 500 for data Postgres can never store (#6446, thanks @alessandropcostabr). A tool result with a NUL character (for example from reading a binary file) failed every time, and a 500 sent it to the local spool to be retried until the window expired. NUL is now removed from payload and metadata before private-tag stripping and redaction, and other data exceptions (SQLSTATE class 22) answer 400 InvalidData.

My Work and API agents

  • OpenAI-compatible observers and API agents can send an explicit reasoning effort (#6434, thanks @ZedingZhang, closes #6433 reported by @maxmad64bis). Set CLAUDE_MEM_OPENAI_COMPAT_REASONING_EFFORT to none, low, medium, high, xhigh or max in the viewer, settings file or environment. Leaving it empty keeps each caller's current behavior. If an endpoint rejects the field, the client resends once without it and remembers that for the URL.
  • My Work API refresh timeout and answer limit are configurable (#6437, thanks @ZedingZhang, part of #6432 reported by @hello-sebastian). With CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_AGENT=api, CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_API_TIMEOUT_MS (default 600000, 10 minutes) sets the deadline for one refresh question, and CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS (default 16000) caps one answer. A cut-off answer reports the cap it used.
  • A story preference one character over the cap no longer discards the whole story (#6435, thanks @GratefulDave). On the retry, a preference over 200 characters is now cut at a word boundary, like the review steps already do. The first answer is still checked strictly.

Translations

  • Brazilian Portuguese now covers the viewer, settings and setup (#6436, thanks @ZedingZhang, closes #6318 reported by @dev-a4). Selecting Português (Brasil) translates the classic viewer, settings and setup alongside My Work.

Internal

  • Owner-only file mode assertions are skipped on Windows, which has no POSIX permission bits (#6443, thanks @Lupin133).
  • The backup restore test suites pass on Windows (#6444, thanks @Lupin133).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v16.1.2...v16.1.3

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v19.2.0 Stable2026-11-25T08:32:28Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Added

  • Added customizable notices for unmet soft tool requirements, allowing hosts to specify the text shown when a required tool call does not satisfy its requirement.
  • Added AgentLoopError and turn-level error metadata so hosts can distinguish an agent loop that ended because a soft tool requirement was not met from a failed request.
  • Added abort-signal support to provider-context transformation and side-request context building.
  • Added optional tool detachment when aborting an agent run, allowing tools that support background execution to continue instead of being terminated.
  • Agents can accept an asynchronous Cursor handler factory; a session opens its execution bridge on the first Cursor request, including after a model switch.

Fixed

  • Fixed DeepSeek DSML markup leaking into tool-call arguments and being executed or replayed.
  • Fixed DeepSeek DSML markup being incorrectly included in conversation history on OpenRouter Responses requests.
  • Fixed unmet soft tool requirements leaving tool calls unanswered when a run was continued or retried.

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Breaking Changes

  • maxInFlightRequests limits are now scoped per process unless the host configures a ProviderSlotBroker; the previous cross-process lease-directory mechanism has been removed.

Added

  • Added ProviderSlotBroker support through configureProviderSlotBroker(broker), allowing hosts such as coding-agent to coordinate maxInFlightRequests limits across processes.
  • Added AssistantMessage.errorFromAgentLoop to distinguish errors caused by the agent loop from errors returned by the provider.
  • OpenAI- and Anthropic-compatible requests to custom hosts now include an x-session-id header when the host does not provide its own session header, preserving conversation affinity through gateways and proxies. Official OpenAI, Anthropic, and Codex APIs are unchanged.
  • Added an "Anthropic (Claude Pro/Max, 1-year token)" login that lasts a year instead of 30 days and never refreshes; image uploads to Anthropic's Files API stay with the regular login.
  • Added regional and custom endpoint selection to /login otari, storing non-US endpoints with the account so EU and self-hosted deployments do not need OTARI_BASE_URL configured separately.

Changed

  • Requests waiting for maxInFlightRequests capacity now begin immediately when capacity is released, rather than relying on periodic polling.
  • omp auth-gateway now preserves Codex session and thread headers and uses Claude Code and OpenCode session identifiers for prompt-cache affinity, improving cache reuse through the gateway.
  • Anthropic (Claude Pro/Max) logins now refresh the way Claude Code 2.1.332 does and are disabled only when Anthropic rejects the refresh token with invalid_grant; a failed background refresh no longer fails requests while the access token is still valid, and a 403 "OAuth token has been revoked" refreshes the same login before switching accounts.
  • Reduced CPU spent scanning streamed DeepSeek prose and long inline code spans that quote template tokens.

Fixed

  • Fixed generate_image edits through OpenAI-compatible gateways that rejected the documented JSON image-edit format.
  • Anthropic OAuth accounts explicitly rejected by an organization are now excluded from account rotation for seven days, including across sessions and restarts.
  • Fixed text-encoded tool calls from DeepSeek, Kimi K2, and Qwen models on OpenRouter Responses-compatible routes, allowing them to stream as normal tool calls instead of failing or triggering retries.
  • Fixed GitHub Copilot Gemini requests failing when MCP tool schemas contain union types such as ["string", "null"].
  • Reduced duplicate retries for openai-completions streams that receive an HTTP 500 response.
  • Fixed omp usage for Synthetic weekly-only plans so it displays weekly credit usage and the next regeneration time.
  • Fixed antigravity-cli quota exhaustion and unavailable-model handling so affected accounts are correctly rotated or blocked and reported reset times are honored.
  • Fixed Gemini models on OpenRouter losing reasoning during long tool-call loops.
  • Fixed prompt-cache misses for /btw and other side turns on OpenCode Go and Zen.
  • Fixed antigravity-cli losing conversation context during OAuth refreshes and when restoring saved turns after generation metadata compaction.

@oh-my-pi/browser-relay

Fixed

  • Fixed relay sessions leaving an unwanted “omp” tab group in Chrome’s bookmarks bar after closing relay tabs.

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added Otari credential helpers and endpoint-aware model discovery, supporting stored { token, baseUrl } credentials and US/EU endpoints.
  • Added GLM-5.4 to the Zhipu Coding Plan with image input, a 1M-token context window, and low/high/max thinking levels; it is preferred for the slow role over GLM-5.3.
  • Added OpenRouter chat-model discovery for tool-capable models through the OpenAI-compatible provider-models API.
  • Added Gemini-compatible tool schema conversion for OpenAI-compatible providers; GitHub Copilot Gemini models now use the appropriate schema format.

Changed

  • Updated the judge role for OpenCode Go and Zen to prefer OpenCode's free jev-1.13-free model, with Zen's jev-1.14 as a fallback.
  • Changed ai&'s default model to moonshotai/kimi-k3, replacing the unavailable moonshotai/kimi-k2.7-code.
  • Classified OpenCode Zen's Jev 1.14, Gemini Decision 1, GPT-6 Luna Decision, and future decision models as judge models rather than chat models.
  • Removed StepFun's dr-search-api web-search SKU from chat-model listings.
  • Updated Devin and Cursor pricing and usage accounting for image tokens and long-context GPT-5.6 models to match their published rates and limits.
  • Repeated provider resolution reuses bundled model fingerprints instead of serializing the same catalog rows again.

Fixed

  • Improved QwenCloud Token Plan discovery errors by reporting the endpoint host and underlying failure reason when the models endpoint cannot be loaded.
  • Fixed Bedrock Kimi K3.1 requests failing because of unsupported temperature settings, and fixed Kimi K3.1 Pro Highspeed requests failing due to invalid output-token and reasoning-effort settings.
  • Fixed Claude models on Google Vertex failing after the active tool set changed during a session.
  • Fixed Claude models on OpenRouter preserving thinking and reasoning context across follow-up requests and tool calls.
  • Fixed Llama 3.x models served through vLLM-based hosts failing after multiple tool calls in one turn.
  • Fixed Devin accounts belonging to separate organizations under the same email address being merged.
  • Fixed GPT-6.1 Terra sending an unsupported reasoning level when thinking was disabled.
  • Fixed GPT-6.2 and later OpenAI Responses models losing reasoning context after session pruning.
  • Fixed GitHub Copilot model listings to respect the account's available and enabled model roster, including after restarts.
  • Fixed Codex models discovered before their bundled definitions were available displaying slug-style names instead of their normal display names.

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • Migrated extension, hook, and custom-tool UI rendering to the host's @oh-my-pi/pi-tui implementation. Renderers now return pi-tui nodes or row objects, UI factories receive a lightweight UI host, and renderers receive the host theme and text-style helpers. Extensions that import bundled or alternate pi-tui packages no longer load; see Migrating from pi-tui.
  • Removed ctx.ui.setEditorComponent and getEditorComponent; use the ctx.ui.composer hooks instead. registerComposerShape now accepts a ComposerShape, and tools must return nodes from renderCall and renderResult instead of implementing describeCall and describeResult.
  • Updated ctx.ui.custom options: pi-tui overlay options are no longer supported, fullscreen is a standalone option, and onHandle receives a { closed, close() } handle.
  • The package root no longer re-exports pi-tui components and helpers; use the supported theme service and ansiSpans exports instead.
  • --no-tools now restricts the model and subagents to the built-in tools named by --tools. MCP, extension, custom, memory, and auto-learn tools are unavailable; memory is not read or saved; extensions and LSP do not load; goal and plan modes are unavailable; and -e/--hook/--trusted-extension cannot be combined with this option.

Added

  • Added prewalk escalation controls: prewalk.auto: escalate can front-load a task on a stronger model and return to the starting model for the next task, while prewalk.autoTarget: "@initial" returns to the model and thinking level that began the task. Planner suggestions now report when escalation would occur.
  • Added OpenRouter-backed model discovery with discovery.type: openrouter, including OpenRouter catalog discovery and request formatting for providers that expose their own endpoint and credentials.
  • Added archive evaluation APIs for listing calls and results, searching transcripts and reasoning, computing usage statistics, and reading complete records; archive listings can be narrowed with since and until and can include subagent transcripts.
  • Expanded Vim editing support across prompts and forms, including Normal-mode history navigation and search, clipboard paste/yank controls, slash-command entry, configurable start mode, command display, and Vim editing in dialog fields. Added tui.vimYankToClipboard, tui.vimStartMode, and tui.vimShowCommand.
  • Expanded the shell built-ins with BSD-compatible tail, stat, and time behavior; additional ast-grep, fd, and macOS command compatibility; and support for more platform-compatible process, file, date, and text operations.
  • Added clearer subagent waiting indicators and retry-fallback metadata, including the model-change reason and whether a turn stopped because of an unmet requirement.
  • The bash tool now detects and removes a stray heredoc continuation character when it can safely parse the command, with a notice.

Changed

  • omp usage times Anthropic re-login warnings by the expiry Anthropic reports at login, including 1-year logins, and falls back to the ~30-day estimate for older logins.
  • Rebuilt the interactive UI on @oh-my-pi/pi-tui while preserving the existing appearance and layout, and restored configured key mappings throughout dialogs, pickers, forms, and settings.
  • Eval-defined tools are now disabled by default; enable them with eval.tools.enabled. Cross-instance agent messaging is now enabled by default and can be disabled with task.crossInstance.
  • Vim mode now starts each new prompt according to tui.vimStartMode (Insert by default), and plain y no longer copies to the system clipboard unless tui.vimYankToClipboard is enabled; use "+y or "*y for explicit clipboard yanks.
  • providers.maxInFlightRequests is coordinated across omp processes through the daemon, so queued requests start promptly and capacity is returned immediately when a process exits.
  • Config files are reloaded by polling every 500 ms, improving support for network drives and systems with exhausted file-watch limits. Repository status is refreshed at run boundaries and relevant commands instead of continuously polling.
  • Subagents created with one step now receive that step without additional framing. Shell previews preserve actual &&/|| operators and display directory changes as written.
  • The agent performs narrower, final verification for requested work rather than repeatedly checking between edits, and reports the check and result in its response.
  • Interrupting with queued input backgrounds a running bash command or eval cell instead of cancelling it; interruptMode: wait follows the same behavior. Quitting preserves visible output in terminal history, and pasting followed immediately by Enter submits the pasted text.
  • The model picker uses the full terminal width, and the UI provides clearer live tool-call status, model/effort controls, and cross-instance session titles.
  • /wt now identifies ovnfs checkouts explicitly, and --offline keeps routing, image descriptions, and related auxiliary operations on the active provider.
  • Selective commits preserve the drafted contents and Git filtering for large, binary, and non-text files, while avoiding overwrites when the file changed concurrently.
  • The daemon and package now use less idle memory by loading catalogs, browser relay support, and prompt perturbation data only when needed.
  • The prompt bubble’s Edit & resend button now stops the current response, discards queued input, and rewinds the transcript to before the selected prompt.
  • The eval commit draft exposes its record as the synchronous details() method instead of a details field.
  • Rewinding (Esc Esc, /branch//rewind, a turn's Edit and Retry) branches into a new session again instead of moving within the current one; the session you left stays unchanged and the toast's Undo resumes it.
  • Model hub search prepares its index between input turns and shares fuzzy matches with the provider sidebar.
  • ovnfs checkout guidance now has agents commit in the checkout and land the commits (--rebase when the branch moved, --markers on conflicts), keeping apply for when you want uncommitted edits.
  • Streaming text and tool progress no longer repaint unchanged status-bar content.
  • Reduced repeated model lookups during catalog discovery and model-role selection.
  • Startup can resolve --model @role and --model pi/role without composing the full catalog when the role names an available provider/model.
  • The terminal welcome animation now waits until the composer is ready for input.
  • The working terminal title now updates four times per second by default; an explicit spinner interval still controls both the title and the TUI.
  • Waiting on unchanged background jobs no longer polls for progress; the wait card's visible elapsed clocks continue to run.
  • /model opens faster after the session's model picker has warmed up.
  • Reduced CLI bootstrap work by loading session construction, the Cursor execution bridge, peer mesh, and persisted-agent support when needed.
  • Opening a full model catalog on subsequent launches reuses unchanged catalog data instead of rebuilding it.
  • The ask dialog submits with questions left unanswered instead of reopening them; an unchecked multi-select could previously trap Enter between questions, and collab guests now get Next on a multi-select before checking anything.
  • ask forms no longer block Submit on required, pattern, min/max and similar rules: broken answers are flagged as you type and still sent, so you can reply to the question instead of satisfying it.
  • --continue (and auto-resume) now resumes the newest session that no other running omp process has open, instead of starting a new session when the most recent one is open elsewhere.

Fixed

  • Fixed image handling across model switches: vision models receive images, text-only models receive reusable descriptions, and compaction, branching, resumes, and advisor reviews preserve the appropriate representation.
  • Fixed numerous Tern and terminal UI regressions, including missing attachment thumbnails and icons, media viewers, prompt-cache details, ask/approval controls, autocomplete placement, key hints, symbol presets, tight layout, subagent navigation, draft selection, clipboard input, and copy/expand behavior.
  • Fixed submission and interaction failures so failed submissions restore the draft instead of exiting, queued input and attachments are not lost or misapplied, pending approvals can be edited safely, and keyboard and pointer actions work across forms and views.
  • Fixed plan-mode cleanup, interruption notes, model/thinking restoration, automatic compaction fallbacks, and resume behavior so stale planning or reasoning instructions are not sent after the mode ends and oversized handoffs do not repeat indefinitely.
  • Fixed model selection and routing across enabled-model scopes, presets, provider order, ACP sessions, session restore, upstream pins, and auto-thinking transitions. Model catalogs now retain extended context metadata and refresh appropriately in long-running sessions.
  • Improved provider and authentication resilience: restricted subagents and omp usage identify OAuth accounts limited by usage or organization policy; MCP OAuth can request missing scopes and retry after approval; strict and newer MCP handshake variants connect successfully; and OpenRouter credit-limit responses retry after the provider's requested delay.
  • Fixed /usage synchronization in npm installs and when the statistics database is temporarily locked, while retaining available history. Fixed /fast to report when the selected model does not support service tiers.
  • Fixed extension, plugin, and SDK compatibility issues, including host-package resolution, Claude plugin variables and tool mappings, project-scoped discovery, --no-extensions isolation, and importing the published SDK from npm.
  • Fixed language-server startup, timeout, duplicate-process, diagnostics, TypeScript resolution, and file-watch behavior, including safer handling of inotify limits and linked worktrees or submodules.
  • Fixed shell compatibility and state isolation across macOS and other platforms, including set -e, working-directory changes, process IDs, ACLs, timestamps, uptime, top, ps, find, sed, grep, and removal of iCloud placeholder folders.
  • Fixed tool and file-operation issues involving heredocs, ask prefilled HTML-like text, AST searches and edits across symlinks and multiple targets, Magic Edit retries and syntax checks, selective commits, binary content, and format-on-write.
  • Fixed MCP and browser behavior, including streamable HTTP handshakes, 403 scope authorization, browser downloads on Windows, media reads, SDK session settings, and browser API validation.
  • Fixed prompt, transcript, and context correctness issues, including context-token accounting, signed-thinking prompt caches, OpenAI Responses tool results, TTSR interruptions, collab titles, archive/session history, background-job outcomes, and advisor truncation.
  • Fixed safety and control-flow issues: refused or pending tool calls are not replayed on retries, selective commits do not modify HEAD or staging when the index is locked, models no longer commit without being asked, and failed commands provide accurate state and error information.
  • Improved diagnostics for provider failures, model discovery, truncated search results, language-server timeouts, and shell resets so errors identify the relevant command, provider, executable, request, or next action.
  • Sloppy edits now recover common file-header typos and unambiguous update-only patch hunks instead of asking the model to resend an edit that already names its target.
  • A broadcast from the main agent now interrupts its waiting subagents: it reaches them as a parent steer (or interrupt) instead of sitting queued until their current wait window elapses. Broadcasts between peers still queue without interrupting.
  • Fixed the shared mnemopi embedding server and local tiny-model workers staying loaded for as long as any session that used them stays open; they now unload 15 minutes after their last request (#20452)
  • LSP idleTimeoutMs now shuts down idle language servers even while other processes keep changing files in the workspace; forwarded watched-file changes no longer count as activity (#20451).
  • Opening /model reuses fresh provider catalogs instead of fetching them again. F5 still forces a provider refresh.
  • Restored the composer's -> / => queue shorthand display: typing the prefix opens a dim Queueing ➤ header line again, sequential list markers (1., 2., …) are highlighted, and Tern keeps the dimmed prefix inline.

Removed

  • Removed fileToUri, uriToFile, and EquivalentUriMap from @oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent/lsp/utils; import them from @oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent/lsp/uri instead.
  • Hindsight forks now retain only turns added by the fork, rather than the inherited parent transcript.

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Breaking Changes

  • Removed the watch and HEAD_WATCH_INTERVAL_MS exports from @oh-my-pi/pi-natives/vcs; reread repository state when needed instead.

Added

  • Added @oh-my-pi/pi-natives/file-poll with pollFile(file, onChange, intervalMs) for detecting file metadata changes, including atomic saves and symlink retargets.
  • Added truncateToTokens(input, maxTokens) for safely truncating text to a token budget without splitting UTF-16 surrogate pairs.
  • Added repairHeredocContinuations(script) to repair supported shell here-document continuations.
  • Added commitReconciled() for publishing private-index commits safely when the shared index is locked.
  • Added transcript analysis APIs—transcriptCalls(), transcriptGrep(), transcriptStats(), and transcriptMessages()—for inspecting tool calls, reasoning, message content, matches, and call statistics across session transcripts.

Changed

  • OAuth callback handling now polls for callbacks at regular intervals instead of relying on directory watching.
  • Improved tail -f reliability for moved files, symlinks, network drives, and file-watcher failures by combining event-driven updates with polling fallback.
  • Persistent shell sessions no longer carry set -e into subsequent commands; other shell options continue to persist.
  • ShellRunResult now reports whether a command changed the working directory (movedCwd) or left errexit enabled (errexit).

Fixed

  • Fixed PTY sessions and child-process cleanup consuming native blocking-pool threads, which could prevent new terminals and other blocking operations from starting after enough terminals were open.
  • Fixed the Linux shell open command creating an excessive D-Bus thread pool on first use.
  • Fixed Windows Chromium and Electron captures and accessibility trees incorrectly reporting fully covered windows as current when covered by popup, tool, or click-through windows.
  • Fixed standalone binaries failing to start when the natives directory was read-only or owned by another user despite already containing the extracted addon.

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • Added @oh-my-pi/pi-tui, a terminal UI with a unified Tern Surface Protocol node tree, native and terminal rendering, reactive signals, compiler-free JSX, role-based painters and widgets, terminal input and capability support, multiplexer and image handling, resize policies, notifications, Markdown and text utilities, themes, and terminal testing utilities.
  • Added checkout event support to TSP connections and hosts.
  • Added label to editor terminal decoration ranges: replacement text drawn in place of a range on rows where it fits and the caret is outside it.

Changed

  • Hosts now report when the terminal displays the checkout itself through checkoutShown.
  • Theme files are polled for changes, keeping live reload working when editors replace files during save.
  • Terminal animations now skip unchanged frames and share redraws with streaming content; shimmer motion runs at 20 fps.

Fixed

  • Fixed streaming Tern content being unnecessarily redrawn as newly inserted when content above it changes.
  • Fixed keyboard events used to open a page from being delivered to the newly opened page as well.
  • Fixed detection of LaTeX blocks beginning with \begin.
  • Fixed Cmd+A selection in terminal-forwarded editors and inputs so typing, deletion, and paste replace the selected text.
  • Fixed native overlays losing alignment with their anchored fields when the fields move or share local keys.
  • Fixed native menu and tree interactions being delivered to the wrong control or branch, and prevented clicks behind modal sheets from moving focus outside the modal.
  • Fixed terminal-row modal sheets allowing interaction with the content behind them, incorrect click and hover targets after scrolling, and mouse settings not being restored after closing fullscreen views.
  • Fixed terminal replies leaking into the shell prompt after returning from a native session, including after fatal errors.
  • Improved handling of large Tern transcript redraws when resuming sessions or returning from a subagent view.

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Fixed

  • Fatal interactive errors now display a concise cause and the path to the full log after terminal cleanup, while piped stderr continues to include the stack trace.
  • Fixed HTML parsing so <textarea> and <title> content is treated as text, preserving literal angle-bracket content while still decoding entities.
  • Child processes created by ptree without an explicit environment now inherit the current Bun environment, including values loaded from .env files after startup, on all platforms.

Removed

  • Removed the public getProviderInFlightRoot() API; provider in-flight limits no longer rely on a lease directory.

@oh-my-pi/pi-wire

Added

  • Added a TSP checkout event to indicate when a pane displays its checkout view.
  • Added a waiting state for agent tray cards when an agent is running but waiting on its own agents or jobs.
  • Extended the TSP toggle event to support the open state used by menus and selectable controls, including the selected item ID when available.

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v0.9.94 Stable2026-11-25T08:24:19Z

HyperFrames v0.9.94

Released on 2026-11-25.

You can render one part of a film in a single pass, in any format and frame rate, with HDR video and shader transitions. Project files, media paths and captured videos stay inside the project, and code that reads media input options by position works again.

Features

  • Studio Server: The render route renders a part of the film when asked for a start and end (d2d527952, #8787).
  • Producer: Render a part of the film with HDR video or shader transitions (66aba4c7a, #8802).
  • Producer: Render a part of the film in one pass, in any format and frame rate (13ef667e6, #8794).

Fixes

  • Parsers: HTML a browser reads differently is refused before it is rewritten (4864ade28, #8760).
  • Studio: A sealed preview no longer shows the frame before the playhead after a save (b975bbed4, #8818).
  • Share: Exclude private paths from portable project files (44d49a521, #8804).
  • Studio: A sealed resize of a size-tweened layer saves a keyframe and stays where dropped (5f70c5e3b, #8799).
  • Studio: The preview opens only the project's compositions (df0297366, #8725).
  • Parsers: Callers that read mediaInputFormatArgs by position get the format list again (fb8e7eba9, #8806).
  • Parsers: Text in a title or textarea can no longer close it when a document is saved (d15883f30, #8805).
  • Engine: A page that keeps throwing looks up each error's throw site once, a few in all (7d9a14418, #8810).
  • Studio: Apply-to-scope writes only the grading attribute, exactly as given (14d582c0f, #8767).
  • Skills: Helper outputs replace a link at their name instead of writing through it (4920a939b, #8667).
  • Studio: A sealed empty box drags on the press right after its select click (614ece8bb, #8772).
  • Studio: The dev Studio serves project files only through its API (92e19cfd4, #8721).
  • Engine: Read only the host's own cgroup layout for memory and CPU limits (182716eaa, #8803).
  • Parsers: Media paths resolve to absolute paths, and only http(s) skip the literal-name check (3339cb8dd, #8740).
  • Player: A sealed player fails a cross-site film without the runtime as unsealed does (fbdfd188c, #8558).
  • Engine: Playlist walk judges an HLS list's segments as one stream (32618ac51, #8798).
  • Studio Server: Delete, cut, group and ungroup decline a script, style or page-shell target (2a03de9f1, #8801).
  • Studio: A sealed preview's timeline shows a composition nested in a sub-composition (6e7c35f3a, #8736).
  • Studio: A timeline delete takes its clips out of a sealed preview at once (02cef8e07, #8719).
  • Studio: An open text edit keeps its typing when a project file changes outside Studio (8c67cd536, #8690).
  • Sdk: A text edit never writes into a script, style or other non-content element (a9d967e1f, #8795).
  • CLI: Captured videos are saved only under a video name the tool chose (8f335ae59, #8750).
  • CLI: A whisper model is named by one plain file-name part, never a path or link (082d6f6ed, #8755).
  • Producer: Auto GPU mode measures its choice once per machine and reuses it (d55ecae30, #8788).
  • Engine: A render keeps and logs the page's console and error text only up to a fixed size (37b8f2647, #8727).
  • Producer: A private project file stays private under any path spelling (ff34919fb, #8786).
  • CLI: Capture checks the page's token result before using it (c7e19fdd2, #8766).
  • Studio: An element description keeps only a selector Studio would have built (32681eedc, #8789).

Internal

  • Deps: Bump vite to 6.4.6 (934de7b9b, #8815).
  • A PR author's own comment can carry the Studio and player captures (559ad7b6d, #8800).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.9.93...v0.9.94

v0.9.93 Stable2026-11-25T02:41:38Z

HyperFrames v0.9.93

Released on 2026-11-25.

Telemetry turns off the same way everywhere with DO_NOT_TRACK or HYPERFRAMES_NO_TELEMETRY, and a cached whisper model is used only when its checksum matches. Media commands and playlists read only local, self-contained inputs, and auto GPU mode picks software rendering when it is faster.

Fixes

  • Studio: Words selected in a sealed text edit show the formatting toolbar, as unsealed (d4edc6d14f, #8777).
  • CLI: A cached whisper model is used only once its sha256 matches its pin (5c4f5febbc, #8762).
  • CLI: DO_NOT_TRACK and HYPERFRAMES_NO_TELEMETRY turn telemetry off the same way everywhere (20b020d192, #8781).
  • Producer: Auto GPU mode renders in software when software captures faster (c5ed60fc83, #8785).
  • Studio: Agent prompts keep composition values in quoted fields, after the person's request (25ad0d1f08, #8779).
  • Core: A sealed snapshot carries the tweens GSAP's global timeline runs (ab7f81c8a6, #8774).
  • Core: A colour-graded video no longer adds a stray row to the Studio timeline (b31b148f74, #8776).

HyperFrames v0.9.92

Released on 2026-11-24.

A Studio rename now names the files it left on the old path, and sealed previews save moves and resizes like unsealed ones. Renders no longer stop on a URL Node cannot parse, and local media, LUT paths and pasted edits stay where they belong.

Features

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

macOS and Linux:

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

cua-driver-rs-v0.62.0cua-driver-rs: v0.62.0 Prerelease2026-11-24T19:41:34Z

Cua Driver 0.62.0

Summary

This release adds 1 features and includes 2 fixes.

Features

  • expose link url in get_window_state elements. (#8674) Thanks @rrmars.

Fixes

  • map DIP element frames onto X11 window screenshots. (#8373) reported by @nick-inkeep.
  • refuse page query_dom selectors the macOS AX fallback cannot apply. (#7801) reported by @ymehili.

Contributors

Thanks to @nick-inkeep, @rrmars, @ymehili for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

v2.2.3 Stable2026-11-24T20:33:06Z

Fixes

  • fix: CTX_REPLACE_TRIGGER_BYTES is no longer read; the replace trigger is the constant 4096 and doctor names a leftover value (ADR-0027 D1)
  • fix: remember a lost replace bet per session, ADR-0027 D3 (ADR-0027 D3)
  • fix: ctx_search reads a source in order when no query word is in it, so a replaced MCP document comes back in full pages (#1372)
  • fix: OMP doctor, setup and uninstall read OMP's plugin store and agent dir the way OMP does (#1415)
  • fix: codex setup list check reads codex mcp get context-mode --json and names a timeout (#1413)
  • fix: session-scoped unit recall and a lock-free prompt path (#1401)
  • fix: ctx_index and ctx_execute(file=) accept the folders the host added to the session (#1398)

Fission-AI/OpenSpec ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.2.0v2.2.0 - The /openspec skill router Stable2026-11-24T19:55:16Z

What's New in v2.2.0

OpenSpec now has a skill router. Type /openspec, say what you want, and the router picks the right OpenSpec skill and runs it.

How to upgrade

Run openspec update. The update adds the openspec router skill and changes no other skill.

New: the /openspec skill router

  • One command for everything - /openspec accepts any OpenSpec request in plain words. In Codex the command is $openspec.
  • The router picks the skill - You no longer need to know which of the 12 workflow skills to call.
  • The router answers questions - Ask "where does add-auth stand?" and the router checks your project, then tells you the status and the next step.
  • The router runs several steps in order - Ask "verify add-auth, then archive it" and the router runs both skills, pausing wherever a step needs your approval.
  • Every existing skill still works - /openspec-propose and the other 11 skills keep their names, their tab completion and their behavior.
  • Every profile gets the router - Core and custom profiles both install the router, and openspec config profile does not list it because the router is not a workflow you choose.

New: reference files for skills

  • A skill can now ship a references/ folder - The folder sits next to SKILL.md and holds detail the agent opens only when a task needs it.
  • The router is the first skill with references - openspec init and openspec update install, refresh and remove reference files together with their skill.

Improved

  • openspec update repairs reference files - A reference file that was deleted or edited is restored on the next openspec update.

Fixed

  • Windows install no longer fails on a locked file - The install script now retries for up to five seconds when Windows still has the new openspec.exe locked.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v2.1.1...v2.2.0

View release →

pbakaus/impeccable ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
skill-v4.11.0Skill 4.11.0 Stable2026-11-24T19:11:40Z

  • Every save says where it went. A critique, audit or scan ends with Saved to Impeccable and its link, or says plainly why it wasn't saved.

  • Critiques carry their pictures. A saved critique names the page it viewed, so Impeccable can show that page on desktop and phone beside the findings.

  • Taste for the kind of work. Your agent reads the design rules for what it is making (web, slides or motion), not all of them at once.

View release →

cli-v4.11.0CLI 4.11.0 Stable2026-11-24T19:10:29Z

  • Every upload names its page. Critiques, audits, scans and accepted prototypes print Saved to Impeccable with a link, or say plainly why they weren't saved. live-poll --json puts the link on the accept event.

  • Critiques arrive whole. A saved critique carries its page's title, every heuristic's score and all four severity counts. Afterwards, in the background, Impeccable takes a desktop and a phone picture of the page and refreshes the project's Design health and Foundations, at most once a day. Nothing waits on either.

  • Two clones, two names. A project with no remote also sends the name of the folder it sits in, so two checkouts called the same thing can be told apart. Never a path, and never your home folder's name.

  • Taste for the kind of work. impeccable taste shows the design rules for web, slides or motion work, and impeccable taste note records a new one. It says when your own taste is paused.

  • Loop in Cursor, Copilot and opencode. loop connect --agent cursor, copilot or opencode adds Impeccable to that agent, and with Loop 0.2.0 the agent wakes when you answer. loop disconnect puts your files back as they were.

  • Codex trusts Impeccable's hooks. loop connect --agent codex trusts the hooks it adds, so Codex no longer asks you to review them. Your own hooks stay as you left them.

  • Sign in from your agent. Signed out, impeccable mcp offers your agent a sign-in tool, and its other tools appear once you've signed in. The tool list never waits on a sign-in.

  • Sharper Foundations. Comments and switched-off error capture no longer count as evidence, and legal pages and error capture beside a web app's folder do.

  • Fixes. Hooks install correctly on Windows and for Codex, Cursor, GitHub, Grok and Gemini. Live mode wraps components that render another tag, and pauses its poll after three timeouts in a row. Critiques save under the target's project root. Static findings report their element's line. A failed engine download shows curl's error and leaves no stale files behind.

View release →
